# 补天逐模块、逐算法、逐分支实现附录

本附录从当前源码AST提取。函数签名、条件、返回、异常、循环、SQL与调用名均可回到行号复核；它不将静态存在视为运行覆盖或人工语义审阅。赋值顺序、上下文管理器、动态派发和宿主行为仍以源码为准。完整源码留在仓库，散列用于识别本附录对应的实现。已核验核心路径的人工作用、事务与失败语义见《核心实现语义说明.md》；其他模块仍需逐项人工确认。

## butian/adapters/config.py

源码 SHA-256：`de1afb249186ba6d018792985ce461ab1fa68ad506a8b0d567b2341a39f49336`；142行。

模块级说明未提供，以下记录实际接口与控制分支。

### _context_window（L33—L39）

```python
_context_window(entry: dict[str, Any]) -> int | None
```

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

- L35: `value is None`
- L37: `isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, int) or value <= 0`

**显式异常**

- L38: `ValueError('context_window_invalid')`

**返回表达式**

- L36: `None`
- L39: `value`

**调用依赖**：`ValueError`、`entry.get`、`isinstance`。

### _resolve_api_key（L41—L51）

```python
_resolve_api_key(entry: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None
```

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

- L42: `'api_key' in entry and entry['api_key']`
- L45: `env_name`
- L47: `not value`

**显式异常**

- L49: `ValueError(f'api_key_env_not_set:{env_name}')`

**返回表达式**

- L43: `str(entry['api_key'])`
- L50: `value`
- L51: `None`

**调用依赖**：`ValueError`、`entry.get`、`os.environ.get`、`str`。

### _build_provider（L54—L84）

```python
_build_provider(entry: dict[str, Any]) -> Any
```

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

- L56: `ptype not in KNOWN_TYPES`
- L59: `ptype == 'openai-compatible'`
- L61: `not base_url`
- L70: `ptype == 'ollama'`
- L73: `ptype == 'local-command'`
- L75: `not isinstance(command, list) or not command`
- L78: `ptype == 'manual'`
- L82: `not isinstance(command, list) or not command`

**显式异常**

- L57: `ValueError(f"unknown_provider_type:{ptype or 'missing'}")`
- L62: `ValueError('base_url_required')`
- L76: `ValueError('command_required')`
- L83: `ValueError('command_required')`

**返回表达式**

- L63: `OpenAICompatibleProvider(str(base_url), api_key=_resolve_api_key(entry), provider_id=str(entry.get('provider_id') or 'openai-compatible'), model=str(entry.get('model') or 'unknown'), context_window=_context_window(entry))`
- L72: `OllamaProvider(str(base_url), model=str(entry.get('model') or 'unknown'), context_window=_context_window(entry))`
- L77: `LocalCommandProvider([str(item) for item in command], timeout=float(entry.get('timeout', 300.0)))`
- L79: `ManualProvider()`
- L84: `McpProvider([str(item) for item in command], timeout=float(entry.get('timeout', 30.0)), context_window=_context_window(entry))`

**调用依赖**：`LocalCommandProvider`、`ManualProvider`、`McpProvider`、`OllamaProvider`、`OpenAICompatibleProvider`、`ValueError`、`_context_window`、`_resolve_api_key`、`entry.get`、`float`、`isinstance`、`str`、`str(entry.get('type', '')).strip`。

### load_provider_config（L87—L104）

```python
load_provider_config(path: str | Path | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]
```

Read and structurally validate a provider config JSON file.

- L89: `path is None`
- L92: `not file_path.exists()`
- L96: `not isinstance(data, dict)`
- L99: `not isinstance(providers, list)`
- L102: `not isinstance(entry, dict)`

- L101: `(index, entry) in enumerate(providers)`

**显式异常**

- L97: `ValueError('provider_config_must_be_object')`
- L100: `ValueError('providers_must_be_list')`
- L103: `ValueError(f'provider_entry_{index}_must_be_object')`

**返回表达式**

- L90: `[]`
- L93: `[]`
- L104: `providers`

**调用依赖**：`Path`、`ValueError`、`data.get`、`enumerate`、`file_path.exists`、`file_path.open`、`isinstance`、`json.load`。

```

### build_registry（L111—L125）

```python
build_registry(config: list[dict[str, Any]] | None) -> tuple[ProviderRegistry, list[ProviderConfigError]]
```

Build a registry from config entries. Invalid entries are reported, never silently skipped.

Manual provider is always registered so a human can execute tasks even with empty config.

**循环**

- L118: `(index, entry) in enumerate(config or [])`

**异常处理**

- L122: `(ValueError, KeyError, TypeError)`

**返回表达式**

- L125: `(registry, errors)`

**调用依赖**：`ManualProvider`、`ProviderConfigError`、`ProviderRegistry`、`_build_provider`、`entry.get`、`enumerate`、`errors.append`、`registry.register`、`str`。

### load_registry_from_env（L128—L142）

```python
load_registry_from_env(home: Path) -> tuple[ProviderRegistry, list[ProviderConfigError], str | None]
```

Resolve config location: BUTIAN_PROVIDERS_FILE env var first, then <home>/providers.json.

**条件与断言**

- L132: `env_path`
- L134: `not Path(env_path).exists()`
- L138: `default.exists()`

**显式异常**

- L135: `ValueError(f'butian_providers_file_not_found:{env_path}')`

**返回表达式**

- L142: `(registry, errors, path)`

**调用依赖**：`Path`、`Path(env_path).exists`、`ValueError`、`build_registry`、`config_path_for_home`、`default.exists`、`load_provider_config`、`os.environ.get`、`str`。

### _unit（L8—L12）

```

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

### environment_drift（L15—L20）

```python
environment_drift(reference: Mapping[str, object], current: Mapping[str, object]) -> float
```

Hamming drift over explicitly comparable environment keys; absent keys are unknown.

**条件与断言**

- L18: `not keys`

**返回表达式**

- L19: `0.0`
- L20: `sum((reference[key] != current[key] for key in keys)) / len(keys)`

**调用依赖**：`len`、`set`、`sum`。

### multiplicative_freshness（L23—L46）

```python
multiplicative_freshness(*, age_days: float, half_life_days: float, consecutive_failures: int=0, contradiction_count: int=0, environment_drift_score: float=0.0, dependency_drift_score: float=0.0, failure_rate: float=0.35, contradiction_rate: float=0.55) -> float
```

Independent attenuation factors are multiplied; correlated causes must be supplied once.

**条件与断言**

- L35: `not all((isfinite(value) for value in (age_days, half_life_days, consecutive_failures, contradiction_count)))`
- L37: `age_days < 0 or half_life_days <= 0 or consecutive_failures < 0 or (contradiction_count < 0)`

### transition_phase（L48—L69）

```

Explicit spiral transitions; no phase jump without observable evidence.

**条件与断言**

- L54: `regression_evidence and contradiction and (phase in {SkillPhase.TRANSCENDING, SkillPhase.INTEGRATING})`

- L62: `phase == SkillPhase.STABLE and (contradiction or novel_environment) and (state.level >= 0.5)`
- L64: `phase == SkillPhase.ACTIVATING and independent_successes >= 2`
- L66: `phase == SkillPhase.INTEGRATING and composition_successes >= 2`

**返回表达式**

- L69: `SkillState(next_phase, min(1.0, max(0.0, state.level)), max(0, state.abstraction_level + int(next_phase == SkillPhase.TRANSCENDING) - int(phase == SkillPhase.TRANSCENDING and next_phase != phase)), max(0.0, marginal), min(1.0, max(0.0, 1 - marginal / 0.05)) if phase == SkillPhase.DEVELOPING else state.plateau_score, activation_count)`

**调用依赖**：`SkillState`、`int`、`max`、`min`。

### expected_information_gain（L157—L187）

```python
expected_information_gain(prior: Sequence[float], posteriors: Sequence[Sequence[float]], outcome_probabilities: Sequence[float]) -> float
```

Expected entropy reduction across mutually exclusive observations.

The caller must provide a normalized prior, outcome probabilities, and one
normalized posterior per outcome. No outcome or posterior is inferred.

**条件与断言**

- L163: `len(posteriors) != len(outcome_probabilities) or not posteriors`
- L166: `any((not isfinite(value) or value < 0 or value > 1 for value in raw_probabilities))`
- L168: `abs(sum(raw_probabilities) - 1.0) > 1e-09`
- L172: `any((len(posterior) != len(prior) for posterior in posteriors))`
- L177: `probability * posterior_probability > state_probability + 1e-09`
- L182: `abs(mixture - prior_probability) > 1e-09`
- L185: `expected_posterior_entropy > prior_entropy + 1e-09`

**循环**

- L175: `(index, (probability, posterior)) in enumerate(zip(probabilities, posteriors))`
- L176: `(state_probability, posterior_probability) in zip(prior, posterior)`
- L179: `(state, prior_probability) in enumerate(prior)`

**显式异常**

- L164: `ValueError('posterior_outcome_length_mismatch')`
- L167: `ValueError('outcome_probability_out_of_range')`
- L169: `ValueError('outcome_probabilities_must_sum_to_one')`
- L173: `ValueError('posterior_state_length_mismatch')`
- L178: `ValueError(f'posterior_violates_bayes_consistency:{index}')`
- L183: `ValueError('posterior_mixture_must_equal_prior')`
- L186: `ValueError('expected_posterior_entropy_exceeds_prior')`

**返回表达式**

- L187: `max(0.0, prior_entropy - expected_posterior_entropy)`

**调用依赖**：`ValueError`、`abs`、`any`、`entropy`、`enumerate`、`float`、`isfinite`、`len`、`max`、`sum`、`zip`。

### _safe_relative（L26—L34）

```python
_safe_relative(value: str) -> PurePosixPath
```

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

- L27: `not isinstance(value, str) or not value or '\\' in value`

### _is_redirect（L37—L46）

```python
_is_redirect(path: Path) -> bool
```

Reject symlinks and Windows reparse points without following targets.

**异常处理**

- L41: `FileNotFoundError`

**返回表达式**

- L42: `False`
- L43: `stat.S_ISLNK(metadata.st_mode) or bool(getattr(metadata, 'st_file_attributes', 0) & getattr(stat, 'FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT', 1024))`

**调用依赖**：`bool`、`getattr`、`path.lstat`、`stat.S_ISLNK`。

### _scan_tree（L48—L74）

```python
_scan_tree(root: Path, *, allow_manifest: bool, ignore_sidecars: bool=False) -> tuple[list[Path], list[str]]
```

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

- L49: `_is_redirect(root)`
- L58: `_is_redirect(path)`
- L65: `_is_redirect(path) or not path.is_file()`
- L67: `'.backup-staging-' in name or '.restore-staging-' in name`
- L69: `ignore_sidecars and name.endswith(('-wal', '-shm'))`
- L71: `not allow_manifest and name == 'manifest.json' and (path.parent == root)`

**循环**

- L53: `(current, dirnames, filenames) in os.walk(root, topdown=True, followlinks=False)`
- L56: `name in sorted(dirnames)`
- L63: `name in sorted(filenames)`

**显式异常**

- L50: `ValueError('backup_symlink_not_allowed')`
- L59: `ValueError('backup_symlink_not_allowed')`
- L66: `ValueError('backup_symlink_not_allowed')`

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dream_runs (run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, started_at REAL NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, gate_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL, candidate_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, reason TEXT);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hypotheses (hypothesis_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, candidate_id TEXT, statement TEXT NOT NULL, prior_reliability REAL NOT NULL, argument TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, evidence_refs TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]', created_at REAL NOT NULL, updated_at REAL NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS verification_actions (action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, hypothesis_id TEXT NOT NULL, description TEXT NOT NULL, cost REAL NOT NULL, risk REAL NOT NULL, expected_information_gain REAL NOT NULL, authorized INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, status TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL, updated_at REAL NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS verification_queue (queue_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, hypothesis_id TEXT NOT NULL, action_id TEXT NOT NULL, priority REAL NOT NULL, reason TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dream_candidates (candidate_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, run_id TEXT NOT NULL, memory_ids TEXT NOT NULL, content TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dream_materials (memory_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, content_hash TEXT NOT NULL, run_id TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dream_verification_evidence (hypothesis_id TEXT NOT NULL, fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL, action_id TEXT NOT NULL, evidence_ref TEXT NOT NULL, outcome TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(hypothesis_id,fingerprint));
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dream_lock (lock_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(lock_id=1), owner TEXT NOT NULL, acquired_at REAL NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_distillations (distillation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, source_memory_ids TEXT NOT NULL, source_snapshot TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]', summary TEXT NOT NULL, merged_items TEXT NOT NULL, conflicts TEXT NOT NULL, discarded_items TEXT NOT NULL, information_loss REAL NOT NULL, reversible INTEGER NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS memory_abstractions (abstraction_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, fingerprint TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, source_memory_ids TEXT NOT NULL, supports TEXT NOT NULL, counterexamples TEXT NOT NULL, applicability TEXT NOT NULL, failure_conditions TEXT NOT NULL, abstraction_level INTEGER NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL);
```

## butian/runtime/execution_job.py

源码 SHA-256：`fc941dd6d305b79cd1ea3939b2371626460132876e519002591def567f2a3875`；217行。

Windows job ownership. Called only by an explicitly launched executor.

The trusted bootstrap waits for a gate before spawning any user command. The
bootstrap is assigned to a non-breakaway, kill-on-close job before gate release.

## butian/runtime/execution_ledger.py

源码 SHA-256：`f66a7ff6aac955e1c6284294b95d9fa24a4d220a261485fc29a525058bbd8ae5`；138行。

Executor-owned intents and ordered observations, independent of business DB.

**事务与数据操作（静态SQL字面量）**

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS executor_runs (
            execution_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, request_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            executor_id TEXT NOT NULL, instance_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            state TEXT NOT NULL, sequence INTEGER NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS executor_events (
            execution_id TEXT NOT NULL, sequence INTEGER NOT NULL, observation TEXT NOT NULL,
            PRIMARY KEY(execution_id, sequence));
```

**调用依赖**：`db.executescript`、`threading.RLock`。

```python

```

- L37: `not execution_id or len(request_sha256) != 64 or any((c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in request_sha256))`

**事务与数据操作（静态SQL字面量）**

```sql
SAVEPOINT executor_accept
```

```sql
UPDATE executor_runs SET sequence=sequence WHERE execution_id=?
```

```sql
SELECT request_sha256 FROM executor_runs WHERE execution_id=?
```

```sql
INSERT INTO executor_runs VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
```

```sql
INSERT INTO executor_events VALUES (?,?,?)
```

```sql
ROLLBACK TO executor_accept
```

### validate_permit（L36—L99）

```python

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

- L40: `set(permit) != PERMIT_FIELDS`
- L42: `request_digest(request) != request.request_sha256`

- L48: `permit['executor_id'] != capabilities.executor_id or permit['instance_id'] != capabilities.instance_id`
- L50: `permit['capability_sha256'] != capability_digest(capabilities)`
- L53: `not isinstance(permit[name], str) or not permit[name].strip()`
- L56: `isinstance(permit[name], bool) or not isinstance(permit[name], int) or permit[name] < 0`
- L60: `not isinstance(digest, str) or len(digest) != 64 or any((c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in digest))`
- L63: `not isinstance(manifest, dict) or hashlib.sha256(canonical(manifest)).hexdigest() != permit['manifest_sha256']`
- L66: `not isinstance(isolation, dict)`
- L69: `not isinstance(required, list) or any((not isinstance(name, str) or not name for name in required))`
- L73: `manifest.get('permissions') != permit['permissions']`
- L75: `hashlib.sha256(request.stdin).hexdigest() != permit['input_sha256']`
- L77: `request.timeout != permit['timeout'] or request.output_limit != permit['output_limit']`
- L80: `budget is not None and (isinstance(budget, bool) or not isinstance(budget, int) or budget <= 0)`
- L82: `not isinstance(permit['permissions'], list) or any((not isinstance(x, str) for x in permit['permissions']))`
- L85: `not isinstance(auth_id, str) or not auth_id or (not authorization_active(auth_id, permit['authorization_generation']))`
- L92: `not cwd.is_absolute() or cwd.resolve() != cwd or (not cwd.is_dir())`
- L94: `not source.is_absolute() or source.is_symlink() or source.resolve() != source or (not source.is_file())`
- L96: `str(source) not in request.argv`
- L98: `hashlib.sha256(source.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != permit['source_sha256']`

**循环**

- L52: `name in ('member_id', 'task_id', 'tool_id')`
- L55: `name in ('version', 'problem_revision', 'authorization_generation')`
- L58: `name in ('source_sha256', 'manifest_sha256', 'input_sha256', 'capability_sha256')`

**显式异常**

- L41: `ValueError('execution_permit_fields_invalid')`
- L43: `ValueError('execution_request_digest_mismatch')`
- L47: `ValueError('execution_permit_expired')`
- L49: `ValueError('execution_permit_identity_mismatch')`
- L51: `ValueError('execution_permit_capabilities_changed')`
- L54: `ValueError('execution_permit_identity_invalid')`
- L57: `ValueError('execution_permit_revision_invalid')`
- L61: `ValueError('execution_permit_hash_invalid')`
- L64: `ValueError('execution_permit_manifest_mismatch')`
- L67: `ValueError('execution_permit_requirements_invalid')`
- L70: `ValueError('execution_permit_requirements_invalid')`
- L74: `ValueError('execution_permit_permissions_mismatch')`

- L78: `ValueError('execution_permit_budget_mismatch')`
- L81: `ValueError('execution_permit_work_budget_invalid')`
- L83: `ValueError('execution_permit_permissions_invalid')`
- L86: `ValueError('execution_permit_authorization_revoked')`
- L93: `ValueError('execution_permit_cwd_invalid')`
- L95: `ValueError('execution_permit_source_invalid')`

## butian/runtime/execution_self_test.py

源码 SHA-256：`1a4f6b571a8ff246046ef3f47b285f2f7731c3376905e92b7e1102ad6fadb1c2`；77行。

Explicitly requested process-control verification. Never run on discovery.

### verify_process_control（L12—L77）

```python
verify_process_control(owner, work_root: Path) -> dict
```

Only an operator in a permitted environment may invoke this routine.

A refusal is latched; there is no alternate termination API or retry.
The target and its descendant naturally expire after three seconds.

**条件与断言**

- L19: `not work_root.is_dir() or work_root.is_symlink()`
- L43: `not ready.wait(2) or (output != [b'ready\r\n'] and output != [b'ready\n'])`
- L46: `process.job.active_count() < 3`
- L53: `time.monotonic() < deadline and (not process.confirmed_exit())`
- L55: `not process.confirmed_exit()`
- L69: `process is not None`
- L76: `process.confirmed_exit()`

**显式异常**

- L20: `ValueError('executor_self_test_directory_invalid')`
- L44: `RuntimeError('executor_self_test_readiness_unconfirmed')`
- L47: `RuntimeError('executor_self_test_descendant_unconfirmed')`
- L56: `RuntimeError('executor_self_test_stop_unconfirmed')`

**异常处理**

- L60: `(OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError)`

**返回表达式**

- L58: `{'verified': True, 'executor_id': owner.executor_id, 'instance_id': owner.instance_id, 'evidence': 'job_active_count_and_exit', 'artifacts': str(directory)}`
- L62: `{'verified': False, 'executor_id': owner.executor_id, 'instance_id': owner.instance_id, 'error_type': type(exc).__name__, 'error_code': getattr(exc, 'winerror', getattr(exc, 'errno', None)), 'stage': stage, 'reason': str(exc) if isinstance(exc, RuntimeError) and str(exc).startswith('executor_self_test_') else 'executor_self_test_operation_failed', 'readiness_hex': [value.hex() for value in output], 'artifacts': str(directory)}`

**调用依赖**：`(directory / 'bootstrap-stderr.txt').write_bytes`、`OwnedProcess`、`Path`、`RuntimeError`、`ValueError`、`getattr`、`isinstance`、`owner.record_self_test`、`process.close`、`process.confirmed_exit`、`process.job.active_count`、`process.process.stderr.read`、`process.process.wait`、`process.release`、`process.stop`、`reader.start`、`ready.is_set`、`ready.wait`、`str`、`str(exc).startswith`、`tempfile.mkdtemp`、`threading.Event`、`threading.Thread`、`time.monotonic`、`time.sleep`、`type`、`value.hex`、`work_root.is_dir`、`work_root.is_symlink`。

## butian/runtime/exploration_budget.py

源码 SHA-256：`03f241d1dc976543e8b46cf07e72793ad8510dd74208318a7c0bf292810ae5f2`；120行。

Persistent bounded exploration ledger; costs are declared budget units, not money.

A budget reservation does not confer OS, tool or external-state permissions.
Started attempts retain their charge even if execution fails or becomes unknown.

### HypothesisStore.__init__（L31—L68）

```python
HypothesisStore.__init__(self, db: sqlite3.Connection, *, write_lock=None)
```

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

- L66: `'owner_member_id' not in columns`

**循环**

- L64: `table in ('prior_knowledge', 'practice_hypotheses', 'hypothesis_authorizations', 'hypothesis_actions', 'hypothesis_observations')`

**事务与数据操作（静态SQL字面量）**

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS prior_knowledge (
            prior_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, source_kind TEXT NOT NULL, title TEXT NOT NULL,
            claim TEXT NOT NULL, environment TEXT NOT NULL, source_refs TEXT NOT NULL,
            reliability REAL NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'candidate',
            created_at REAL NOT NULL, updated_at REAL NOT NULL,
            owner_member_id TEXT
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS practice_hypotheses (
            hypothesis_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, prior_ids TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL,
            statement TEXT NOT NULL, rationale TEXT NOT NULL, conditions TEXT NOT NULL,
            predictions TEXT NOT NULL, falsifiers TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL,
            prior_reliability REAL NOT NULL, verification_required INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
            created_at REAL NOT NULL, updated_at REAL NOT NULL, owner_member_id TEXT
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hypothesis_authorizations (authorization_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, hypothesis_id TEXT NOT NULL, actor_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, decision TEXT NOT NULL, reason TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL, owner_member_id TEXT);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hypothesis_actions (
            action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, hypothesis_id TEXT NOT NULL, description TEXT NOT NULL,
            cost REAL NOT NULL, risk REAL NOT NULL, information_gain REAL NOT NULL,
            authorization TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL,
            started_at REAL, finished_at REAL, owner_member_id TEXT
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS hypothesis_observations (
            observation_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, hypothesis_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            action_id TEXT NOT NULL, outcome TEXT NOT NULL, evidence_ids TEXT NOT NULL,
            environment TEXT NOT NULL, independent_key TEXT NOT NULL, notes TEXT NOT NULL,
            created_at REAL NOT NULL, owner_member_id TEXT, UNIQUE(hypothesis_id, independent_key)
        );
```

**调用依赖**：`self.db.commit`、`self.db.execute`、`self.db.executescript`、`threading.RLock`。

**事务与数据操作（静态SQL字面量）**

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS knowledge_sources (
            source_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner_member_id TEXT, source_type TEXT NOT NULL, locator TEXT NOT NULL,
            title TEXT NOT NULL, content_hash TEXT NOT NULL, retrieved_at REAL NOT NULL,
            metadata TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'candidate'
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS knowledge_source_snapshots (
            source_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, content BLOB NOT NULL, sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY(source_id) REFERENCES knowledge_sources(source_id)
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS source_claims (
            claim_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner_member_id TEXT, source_id TEXT NOT NULL, claim TEXT NOT NULL,
            conditions TEXT NOT NULL, limitations TEXT NOT NULL, confidence REAL NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'candidate', created_at REAL NOT NULL,
            predictions TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]', falsifiers TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'
        );
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS learning_runs (
            run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner_member_id TEXT, mode TEXT NOT NULL, locator TEXT NOT NULL,
            source_id TEXT, status TEXT NOT NULL, details TEXT NOT NULL,
            started_at REAL NOT NULL, finished_at REAL
        )
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS learning_queue (
            queue_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner_member_id TEXT, url TEXT NOT NULL, title TEXT,
            source_type TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'web', timeout REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,
            max_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1000000, status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',
            attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, last_error TEXT, source_id TEXT,
            worker_id TEXT, lease_token TEXT, lease_until REAL,
            created_at REAL NOT NULL, updated_at REAL NOT NULL
        )
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS learning_queue_events (event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, queue_id TEXT NOT NULL, event_type TEXT NOT NULL, worker_id TEXT, attempts INTEGER NOT NULL, details TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL)
```

```sql
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ux_learning_queue_owner_url_v2 ON learning_queue(COALESCE(owner_member_id, ''), url)
```

**调用依赖**：`schema.split`、`self.db.execute`、`statement.strip`、`threading.RLock`。

```

SELECT status,revision FROM tasks WHERE task_id=?

ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1', (session['task_id'],)).fetchone`、`self.store.db.execute('SELECT status,revision FROM tasks WHERE task_id=?', (session['task_id'],)).fetchone`、`self.store.provider_session`、`session.get`。

### Store.recover（L512—L547）

```python
Store.recover(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]
```

Recover prepared transitions using their durable event intent.

A prepared operation is committed only when the task already reflects the
event or can be advanced from the exact recorded predecessor state. Any
mismatch remains incomplete for manual review; recovery never guesses.

**条件与断言**

- L523: `not event`
- L533: `task and task['status'] == intent['to'] and (task['revision'] == target_revision)`
- L535: `task and task['status'] == intent['from'] and (task['revision'] == target_revision - 1)`
- L538: `recovered`

**循环**

- L521: `operation in pending`

**返回表达式**

- L547: `results`

**事务与数据操作（静态SQL字面量）**

```sql
SELECT * FROM operations WHERE state IN ('prepared','pending_recovery')
```

```sql
SELECT * FROM events WHERE operation_id=? AND event_type='task_transition'
```

```sql
UPDATE operations SET state='incomplete', error=? WHERE operation_id=?
```

```sql
SELECT status, revision FROM tasks WHERE task_id=?
```

```sql
UPDATE tasks SET status=?, revision=? WHERE task_id=? AND status=? AND revision=?
```

```sql
UPDATE operations SET state='committed', error=NULL WHERE operation_id=?
```

```sql
UPDATE events SET commit_state='committed' WHERE operation_id=?
```

```sql
UPDATE events SET commit_state='incomplete' WHERE operation_id=? AND commit_state!='committed'
```

**调用依赖**：`dict`、`int`、`json.loads`、`results.append`、`self.db.execute`、`self.db.execute("SELECT * FROM events WHERE operation_id=? AND event_type='task_transition'", (operation['operation_id'],)).fetchone`、`self.db.execute('SELECT status, revision FROM tasks WHERE task_id=?', (event['aggregate_id'],)).fetchone`。

```python

```

**事务与数据操作（静态SQL字面量）**

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_assets (

            tool_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL,

            description TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, manifest TEXT NOT NULL,

            created_at REAL NOT NULL, updated_at REAL NOT NULL, current_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,

            UNIQUE(owner_member_id, name)

        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_versions (

            tool_id TEXT NOT NULL, version INTEGER NOT NULL, content_hash TEXT NOT NULL,

            source_path TEXT NOT NULL, manifest TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL,

            PRIMARY KEY(tool_id, version)

        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_events (

            event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, tool_id TEXT NOT NULL, version INTEGER,

            event_type TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL, idempotency_key TEXT UNIQUE, created_at REAL NOT NULL

        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_verifications (

            verification_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, tool_id TEXT NOT NULL, version INTEGER NOT NULL,

            task_id TEXT, environment TEXT NOT NULL, outcome TEXT NOT NULL, evidence_ids TEXT NOT NULL,

            independent_key TEXT NOT NULL, execution_id TEXT, input_hash TEXT, expected_output TEXT,
            created_at REAL NOT NULL, UNIQUE(tool_id, version, independent_key)

        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_shares (

            share_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, tool_id TEXT NOT NULL, version INTEGER NOT NULL,

            owner_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, recipient_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, permission TEXT NOT NULL,

            status TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL, issue_id TEXT, max_uses INTEGER, used_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, expires_at REAL,
            UNIQUE(tool_id, version, recipient_member_id)

        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_issues (

            issue_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, tool_id TEXT NOT NULL, version INTEGER NOT NULL,

            reporter_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, execution_id TEXT, description TEXT NOT NULL,

            severity TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, repair_version INTEGER,

            diagnosis TEXT, created_at REAL NOT NULL, updated_at REAL NOT NULL,
            review_member_id TEXT, review_execution_id TEXT, review_evidence TEXT,
            triage_owner_member_id TEXT, triage_reason TEXT, duplicate_of TEXT,
            requested_information TEXT, rejection_reason TEXT, escalated_at REAL,
            due_at REAL, environment TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', input_summary TEXT,
            expected_output TEXT, actual_output TEXT, reproduction_steps TEXT, impact_scope TEXT, escalation_to_member_id TEXT

        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_issue_events (
            event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, issue_id TEXT NOT NULL, event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
            actor_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, from_status TEXT, to_status TEXT, payload TEXT NOT NULL,
            idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, created_at REAL NOT NULL
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_issue_pairs (
            pair_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, issue_id TEXT NOT NULL, repair_version INTEGER NOT NULL,
            environment TEXT NOT NULL, input_hash TEXT NOT NULL, before_outcome TEXT NOT NULL,
            after_outcome TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL,
            before_execution_id TEXT, after_execution_id TEXT,
            UNIQUE(issue_id, repair_version, environment, input_hash)
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_issue_escalations (
            work_item_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, issue_id TEXT NOT NULL, dispatcher_member_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            status TEXT NOT NULL, reason TEXT NOT NULL, claimed_by TEXT, resolution TEXT,
            created_at REAL NOT NULL, claimed_at REAL, completed_at REAL, updated_at REAL NOT NULL
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_issue_escalation_events (
            event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, work_item_id TEXT NOT NULL, event_type TEXT NOT NULL,
            actor_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL, idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, created_at REAL NOT NULL
        );

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS host_authorizations (
            authorization_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, owner_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, tool_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            version INTEGER NOT NULL, capabilities TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL,
            reason TEXT NOT NULL, created_at REAL NOT NULL, revoked_at REAL, revoke_reason TEXT
        );
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_host_auth_tool ON host_authorizations(tool_id, version, owner_member_id, status);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS host_authorization_executors (
            authorization_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, executor_id TEXT NOT NULL, instance_id TEXT NOT NULL
        );
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS host_revocation_outbox (
            authorization_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, delivery_state TEXT NOT NULL,
            attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, last_error_type TEXT,
            created_at REAL NOT NULL, acknowledged_at REAL
        );

        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO host_revocation_outbox (authorization_id,delivery_state,created_at)
        SELECT authorization_id,'pending',COALESCE(revoked_at,created_at)
        FROM host_authorizations WHERE status='revoked';

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_circuit_breakers (
            tool_id TEXT NOT NULL, version INTEGER NOT NULL, scope TEXT NOT NULL,
            reason TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, opened_at REAL NOT NULL,
            recovered_at REAL, recovered_by TEXT, PRIMARY KEY(tool_id, version, scope)
        );

        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tools_owner_status ON tool_assets(owner_member_id, status);

        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_verify ON tool_verifications(tool_id, version, outcome);

        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_executions (

            execution_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, tool_id TEXT NOT NULL, version INTEGER NOT NULL,

            actor_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, task_id TEXT, input_hash TEXT NOT NULL,

            dry_run INTEGER NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, exit_code INTEGER,

            stdout TEXT NOT NULL, stderr TEXT NOT NULL, termination_reason TEXT,

            permissions TEXT NOT NULL, started_at REAL NOT NULL, finished_at REAL,
            runtime_backend TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'python_host', isolation_level TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'host_process',
            isolation_capabilities TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}', business_assertions TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'

        );
```

```sql
CREATE TABLE tool_issue_escalations (
                work_item_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, issue_id TEXT NOT NULL, dispatcher_member_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                status TEXT NOT NULL, reason TEXT NOT NULL, claimed_by TEXT, resolution TEXT,
                created_at REAL NOT NULL, claimed_at REAL, completed_at REAL, updated_at REAL NOT NULL
            )
```

```sql
INSERT INTO tool_issue_escalations
                (work_item_id, issue_id, dispatcher_member_id, status, reason, claimed_by, resolution,
                 created_at, claimed_at, completed_at, updated_at)
                SELECT work_item_id, issue_id, dispatcher_member_id, status, reason, claimed_by, resolution,
                       created_at, claimed_at, completed_at, updated_at
                FROM tool_issue_escalations_legacy
```

```sql
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_issue_escalations_dispatcher_status ON tool_issue_escalations(dispatcher_member_id, status)
```

**调用依赖**：`any`、`self.store.db.execute`、`self.store.db.execute('PRAGMA index_list(tool_issue_escalations)').fetchall`、`self.store.db.execute(f'PRAGMA index_info({index[1]})').fetchall`、`self.store.db.executescript`。

ToolStore.share(self, owner_member_id: str, tool_id: str, recipient_member_id: str, *, permission: str='team_readonly')
```

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

- L2808: `permission not in {'team_readonly', 'team_execute', 'shareable_copy'}`
- L2812: `tool['owner_member_id'] != owner_member_id`
- L2814: `tool['status'] != 'active'`
- L2822: `existing`
- L2824: `existing['permission'] != permission`

**显式异常**

- L2808: `ValueError('invalid_tool_share_permission')`
- L2812: `ValueError('tool_access_denied')`
- L2814: `ValueError('only_active_tool_can_be_shared')`
- L2826: `ValueError('tool_share_already_exists')`

**返回表达式**

- L2828: `dict(existing)`
- L2835: `{'share_id': share_id, 'tool_id': tool_id, 'version': tool['current_version'], 'status': 'shared_candidate', 'permission': permission}`

**事务与数据操作（静态SQL字面量）**

```sql
SELECT * FROM tool_shares WHERE tool_id=? AND version=? AND recipient_member_id=?
```

```sql
INSERT INTO tool_shares (share_id, tool_id, version, owner_member_id, recipient_member_id, permission, status, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
```

**调用依赖**：`ValueError`、`dict`、`self._event`、`self._member_dir`、`self._tool`、`self.store.db.execute`、`self.store.db.execute('SELECT * FROM tool_shares WHERE tool_id=? AND version=? AND recipient_member_id=?', (tool_id, tool['current_version'], recipient_member_id)).fetchone`、`time.time`、`uuid.uuid4`。

### ToolStore._transition（L2839—L2857）

```python
ToolStore._transition(self, tool_id: str, status: str, payload: dict[str, Any], key: str)
```

无函数文档字符串；以下为源码实际控制逻辑。

**条件与断言**

- L2841: `status not in STATUSES`
- L2847: `not current`
- L2851: `status not in _ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get(current['status'], set())`

**显式异常**

- L2843: `ValueError('invalid_tool_status')`
- L2849: `ValueError('tool_not_found')`
- L2853: `ValueError('invalid_tool_status_transition')`

**事务与数据操作（静态SQL字面量）**

```sql
SELECT status FROM tool_assets WHERE tool_id=?
```

```sql
UPDATE tool_assets SET status=?, updated_at=? WHERE tool_id=?
```

**调用依赖**：`ValueError`、`_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS.get`、`self._event`、`self.store.db.execute`、`self.store.db.execute('SELECT status FROM tool_assets WHERE tool_id=?', (tool_id,)).fetchone`、`set`、`time.time`。
